0209 — cable reverse preacher curl

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0209 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: prone.

A preacher curl on a cable with an overhand grip. It targets the brachialis and forearms while the pad keeps you strict.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Adjust the cable machine so that the preacher curl pad is at chest height. Sit on the preacher curl bench and place your upper arms on the pad, with your palms facing down and your elbows fully extended. Grab the cable handles with an underhand grip, shoulder-width apart.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0209.json#/instructions/0, 0209.json#/instructions/1, 0209.json#/instructions/2

Arms Hands

Sit on the preacher curl bench and place your upper arms on the pad, with your palms facing down and your elbows fully extended. Grab the cable handles with an underhand grip, shoulder-width apart.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0209.json#/instructions/1, 0209.json#/instructions/2

Legs Feet

Keep the legs comfortably extended and quiet on the documented support; retain any source-defined leg motion or restraints.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial leg placement was not explicit; this is a reversible balance/support choice, not a replacement for source movement. 0209.json#/instructions/1

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Adjustable cable station, Adjustable utility bench. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0209.json#/instructions/1

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Adjust the cable machine so that the preacher curl pad is at chest height. 0209.json#/instructions/0
  2. Sit on the preacher curl bench and place your upper arms on the pad, with your palms facing down and your elbows fully extended. 0209.json#/instructions/1
  3. Grab the cable handles with an underhand grip, shoulder-width apart. 0209.json#/instructions/2
  4. Keeping your upper arms stationary, exhale and curl the handles towards your shoulders, contracting your biceps. 0209.json#/instructions/3
  5. Pause for a moment at the top of the movement, squeezing your biceps. 0209.json#/instructions/4
  6. Inhale and slowly lower the handles back to the starting position, fully extending your elbows. 0209.json#/instructions/5
  7. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 0209.json#/instructions/6

0210 — cable reverse wrist curl

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0210 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

A seated cable wrist curl with an overhand grip, with the wrists hanging past the thighs. It strengthens the top of the forearms.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Attach a cable to a low pulley and sit on a bench facing the cable machine. Grasp the cable handle with an overhand grip, palms facing down. Rest your forearms on your thighs, with your wrists hanging off the edge.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0210.json#/instructions/0, 0210.json#/instructions/1, 0210.json#/instructions/2

Arms Hands

Grasp the cable handle with an overhand grip, palms facing down. Rest your forearms on your thighs, with your wrists hanging off the edge.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0210.json#/instructions/1, 0210.json#/instructions/2

Legs Feet

For an otherwise unspecified seated setup, place the feet in a stable comfortable support position; preserve any source-defined footrests, pedals, leg pads or floor contact.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial leg placement was not explicit; this is a reversible balance/support choice, not a replacement for source movement. 0210.json#/instructions/0

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0210.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Attach a cable to a low pulley and sit on a bench facing the cable machine. 0210.json#/instructions/0
  2. Grasp the cable handle with an overhand grip, palms facing down. 0210.json#/instructions/1
  3. Rest your forearms on your thighs, with your wrists hanging off the edge. 0210.json#/instructions/2
  4. Keeping your forearms stationary, exhale and curl your wrists upward as far as possible. 0210.json#/instructions/3
  5. Pause for a moment at the top, then inhale and slowly lower your wrists back down to the starting position. 0210.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 0210.json#/instructions/5

0211 — cable russian twists (on stability ball)

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0211 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

Seated on a stability ball, you rotate a cable handle from hip to hip. It trains the abs and obliques and also challenges your balance.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Sit on a stability ball with your feet flat on the ground and your knees bent at a 90-degree angle. Hold the cable handle with both hands and extend your arms straight out in front of you.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0211.json#/instructions/0, 0211.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Hold the cable handle with both hands and extend your arms straight out in front of you.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0211.json#/instructions/1

Legs Feet

Sit on a stability ball with your feet flat on the ground and your knees bent at a 90-degree angle.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0211.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Adjustable cable station, Swiss/stability ball. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0211.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Sit on a stability ball with your feet flat on the ground and your knees bent at a 90-degree angle. 0211.json#/instructions/0
  2. Hold the cable handle with both hands and extend your arms straight out in front of you. 0211.json#/instructions/1
  3. Lean back slightly while keeping your back straight and your core engaged. 0211.json#/instructions/2
  4. Twist your torso to the right, bringing the cable handle towards your right hip. 0211.json#/instructions/3
  5. Pause for a moment, then twist your torso to the left, bringing the cable handle towards your left hip. 0211.json#/instructions/4
  6. Continue alternating twists for the desired number of repetitions. 0211.json#/instructions/5

0212 — cable seated crunch

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0212 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

A seated cable crunch with the handle behind the head. You curl the torso forward against the pull to work the abs.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Sit on a cable machine with your feet flat on the ground and your knees bent. Hold the cable handle with both hands and position it behind your head.

  3. Engage your abs and slowly curl your upper body forward, bringing your chest towards your knees. 0212.json#/instructions/2
  4. Pause for a moment at the top, then slowly return to the starting position. 0212.json#/instructions/3
  5. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 0212.json#/instructions/4

0213 — cable seated high row (v-bar)

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0213 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

A seated cable row from a high position using a v-bar. You pull toward the torso to work the lats and the middle back.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Sit on the cable machine with your feet flat on the floor and your knees slightly bent. Grasp the v-bar attachment with an overhand grip, palms facing each other, and your hands shoulder-width apart.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0213.json#/instructions/0, 0213.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Grasp the v-bar attachment with an overhand grip, palms facing each other, and your hands shoulder-width apart.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0213.json#/instructions/1

Legs Feet

Sit on the cable machine with your feet flat on the floor and your knees slightly bent.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0213.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Adjustable cable station, V/close-grip cable handle. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0213.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Sit on the cable machine with your feet flat on the floor and your knees slightly bent. 0213.json#/instructions/0
  2. Grasp the v-bar attachment with an overhand grip, palms facing each other, and your hands shoulder-width apart. 0213.json#/instructions/1
  3. Keep your back straight and lean slightly forward from the hips. 0213.json#/instructions/2
  4. Pull the v-bar towards your torso by retracting your shoulder blades and squeezing your back muscles. 0213.json#/instructions/3
  5. Pause for a moment at the peak of the contraction, then slowly release the tension and return to the starting position. 0213.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 0213.json#/instructions/5

0214 — cable seated one arm alternate row

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0214 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

A seated cable row that alternates between arms. It works the back one side at a time with a full squeeze each rep.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Sit on a bench facing a cable machine with your feet flat on the ground and knees slightly bent. Grasp the handle with one hand and keep your arm fully extended in front of you.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0214.json#/instructions/0, 0214.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Grasp the handle with one hand and keep your arm fully extended in front of you.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0214.json#/instructions/1

Legs Feet

Sit on a bench facing a cable machine with your feet flat on the ground and knees slightly bent.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0214.json#/instructions/0

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Adjustable cable station, Adjustable utility bench. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0214.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Sit on a bench facing a cable machine with your feet flat on the ground and knees slightly bent. 0214.json#/instructions/0
  2. Grasp the handle with one hand and keep your arm fully extended in front of you. 0214.json#/instructions/1
  3. Pull the handle towards your body, retracting your shoulder blade and keeping your elbow close to your side. 0214.json#/instructions/2
  4. Pause for a moment at the top of the movement, squeezing your back muscles. 0214.json#/instructions/3
  5. Slowly release the handle back to the starting position. 0214.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat with the other arm. 0214.json#/instructions/5
  7. Alternate between arms for the desired number of repetitions. 0214.json#/instructions/6

0215 — cable seated rear lateral raise

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0215 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

A seated cable rear raise: arms sweep out to the sides to the level of the shoulders. It trains the rear delts and upper back.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Sit on a bench facing the cable machine with your feet flat on the ground. Grasp the cable handles with an overhand grip and extend your arms straight in front of you.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0215.json#/instructions/0, 0215.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Grasp the cable handles with an overhand grip and extend your arms straight in front of you.

A close-grip dumbbell press with the weights held together. It places the load on the triceps.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Sit on a flat bench with a dumbbell in each hand, resting on your thighs.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0296.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Sit on a flat bench with a dumbbell in each hand, resting on your thighs. 0296.json#/instructions/0
  2. Using your thighs to help raise the dumbbells, lift the dumbbells one at a time so that you can hold them in front of you at shoulder width. 0296.json#/instructions/1
  3. Once at shoulder width, rotate your wrists forward so that the palms of your hands are facing away from you. This will be your starting position. 0296.json#/instructions/2
  4. As you breathe in, slowly lower the dumbbells to your side until they are about level with your chest. 0296.json#/instructions/3
  5. As you exhale, use your triceps to lift the dumbbells back to the starting position. 0296.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 0296.json#/instructions/5

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand up straight with a dumbbell in each hand, palms facing your torso. 0312.json#/instructions/0
  2. Keep your elbows close to your torso and rotate the palms of your hands until they are facing forward. 0312.json#/instructions/1
  3. This will be your starting position. 0312.json#/instructions/2
  4. Now, keeping the upper arms stationary, exhale and curl the weights while contracting your biceps. 0312.json#/instructions/3
  5. Continue to raise the weights until your biceps are fully contracted and the dumbbells are at shoulder level. 0312.json#/instructions/4
  6. Hold the contracted position for a brief pause as you squeeze your biceps. 0312.json#/instructions/5
  7. Then, inhale and slowly begin to lower the dumbbells back to the starting position. 0312.json#/instructions/6
  8. Repeat for the recommended amount of repetitions. 0312.json#/instructions/7
